## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:45:03Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-requirements
**Details**: A — two-backend custody per BR2.4: keyring-first + defined file fallback, never beside metadata, deleted on every terminal path, zombie rows named not skipped
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:45:04Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-requirements
**Details**: A — bounded output honoring server OUTPUT_MAX + BR7.2 enumerated-value scrub as the client-side egress control; server redactor is second pass
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:45:04Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-requirements
**Details**: A — code-keyed terminal/backoff taxonomy + idempotent reports + exactly-once cancel close-out + signal close-out; loop stays alive on transient failures for supervisor visibility
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:45:04Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-requirements
**Details**: A — client-clamped [1,60] claim wait, +15s claim timeout, 15s heartbeat, 1s cancel-only floor, caps under OUTPUT_MAX; serial executor bounds itself
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:45:04Z

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:56Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — src/pilot/{mod,client,custody,exec,state}.rs + checked-in openapi.yaml; zero new crates, all seams reused
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:56Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — custody.rs two-backend write/read/delete with Backend enum; secrets-map atomic write; status reports backend-not-bytes; targets file stays secret-free
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:57Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — tokio select! loop: heartbeat interval vs claim future; serial exec; RunState holds running/settled/backoff/root; signals ride the same close-out
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:57Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — typed C3Error enum + single terminate(reason) funnel: kill-first, report-or-not per cred liveness, custody deletes, pinned exit codes
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:57Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — pgid-detached spawn + bounded tail buffer + deadline kill + jail-resolve + enumerated scrub; cancel via RunState.running map
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:57Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — root canonicalized once at launch + per-op lexical→canonical→boundary→symlink_metadata chain; config_dir exclusion absolute
**Unit**: connector-client

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-28T08:59:57Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: nfr-design
**Details**: A — wiremock C3 suite + no-network pilot unit tests + docs/help carrying the contract + openapi.yaml; existing CI lanes
**Unit**: connector-client
